- **gcms-spectral-match-filtering**: Use when top-hit GC-MS spectral match annotations (with cosine similarity and balance score columns) from MSHub/GNPS auto-deconvolution need FDR-controlled filtering, e.g. reproducing Figure 1m,n-style threshold benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gcms-spectral-match-filtering))
